Internal FFT:
```
def _fft_score(a):
    L = fft_len
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:m]
    return 2.0*n*C.max(), C
```
Since a sum1; if not exactly, normalize after each update.

Load:
```
def _load(a, lam):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(lam, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A*B, L)
    return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Need `a_rev = a[::-1]` updated each iter. `lam` length m=2n-1; values nonnegative sum1. Convolution length n+m-1 =3n-2 <=L. The segment index: conv[k]=∑ a_rev[j] lam[k-j]. We want load_i as above. Test with n=2, a=[a0,a1], lam=[l0,l1,l2], a_rev=[a1,a0]. conv indices:
k=0: a1*l0
k=1: a1*l1 + a0*l0
k=2: a1*l2 + a0*l1
k=3: a0*l2
We want load_0 = l0*a0 + l1*a1? formula ∑_j a_j l_{j+0}=a0*l0+a1*l1. That's conv[1]. load_1 = a0*l1+a1*l2 = conv[2]. segment starts n-1=1, good. load_i=conv[n-1+i]. Good.

Active weights:
Function:
```
def _lambda_from_C(C, top_frac, p, alpha):
    m=C.size
    k=max(10, int(top_frac*m))
    thresh=np.partition(C, m-k)[m-k]
    # include lags >= thresh
    vals = np.where(C>=thresh, C, 0.0)
    spread=C.max()-thresh
    if spread>1e-18:
        w = (np.clip(vals-thresh,0,None)/spread + 1e-3)**p
    else: w=(vals>0).astype(float)
    # add uniform baseline
    w = w + alpha
    s=w.sum()
    return w/s
```
But adding alpha over all lags length m makes baseline not negligible if k small. We can normalize; alpha maybe 0.05? If w baseline 0.05*9000=450, top weights maybe k*1=1800, okay. Use alpha=0.02. Or better set w=0 and after add small uniform: `w = top_weights/maxtop + alpha`? To keep top importance, scale top to median1. Simpler: `w = (C >= thresh)`, maybe weights proportional `(C-thresh+eps)**p`. Then add `alpha` to all, then normalize. The total baseline alpha*m could dominate if alpha large. Use alpha=0.01. Top weights average 1, top sum~k, baseline~90, if k=900 baseline 10%; okay.

def _clean(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   return a/s
```

def _perturb(a, rng, level):
   # random log noise
   sig = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4][min(level,3)]
   noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,a.size))
   a=a*noise
   if level>=2 and parent is not None:
       blend=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)
       a=(1-blend)*a+blend*parent
   a=_normalize(a)
```

    parent=np.clip(parent,0,1000)

def norm(v):
   v=np.asarray(v,float); v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0); v=np.clip(v,0,1000)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return None
   return v/s

Optimization setup:
```
m=2*n-1
L=1
while L < 3*n-3: L <<=1
# precompute? a_rev changes.
def fft_score(a):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:m]
   # ignore tiny negatives? max(C)
   return float(2.0*n*C.max()), C
def load_fn(a,lam):
   ar=a[::-1]
   A=np.fft.rfft(ar,L)
   Blam=np.fft.rfft(lam,L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*Blam,L)
   return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Computing `lam` FFT every iter; λ length m; L same. Good.

Could use `np.fft.rfft` of lam each iter; length 9000. Fine. We can optimize by using `np.fft.rfft(lam, L)`; lam has nonzero all.

Time guard in functions? Not necessary.

floor=1e-18
# to avoid exact too often, exact every 25 iters or if S < best_score*0.9995
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   it+=1
   S,C=fft_score(a)
   # exact update periodically
   if it%25==0 or S < best_score*0.999:
       sc=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
       if np.isfinite(sc):
           if sc < best_score:
               best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); exact_stall=0
           else:
               exact_stall +=1
   # if exact_stall large, perturb
   if exact_stall > 300:
       a=best_a.copy()  # reset
       a = _perturb(a, rng, 2)
       S,C=fft_score(a)
       exact_stall=100
       v=None
   # if current too far from best, reset
   if S > best_score + 0.05 and it%50==0:
       a=best_a.copy()
       a=_perturb(a,rng,1)
       S,C=fft_score(a); v=None
   # λ
   if C.max()>0:
      z=np.maximum(C,0)/C.max()
      lam=z**q + alpha
   else: lam=np.ones(m)/m
   lam/=lam.sum()
   load=load_fn(a,lam)
   g=a*load
   g-=g.mean()
   sc90=np.percentile(np.abs(g),90)
   if sc90>1e-300: g/=sc90
   g=np.clip(g,-8,8)
   if v is None: v=g.copy()
   else:
      v=0.7*v + 0.3*g
      v-=v.mean()
      vs90=np.percentile(np.abs(v),90)
      if vs90>1e-300: v/=vs90
      v=np.clip(v,-8,8)
   # line search
   hvals=[eta*0.3, eta*0.6, eta, eta*1.6, eta*2.5]
   # maybe unique positive
   best_trial=None; best_trial_S=S
   best_h=None
   for h in hvals:
      if h<=1e-12: continue
      trial=a*np.exp(-h*v)
      trial=np.maximum(trial, floor)
      ts=trial.sum()
      if ts<=0: continue
      trial/=ts
      tS, _ = fft_score(trial)
      # accept slight improvement; tie maybe smaller step
      if tS < best_trial_S - 1e-13:
          best_trial_S=tS; best_trial=trial; best_h=h
   if best_trial is not None:
       a=best_trial
       # adjust eta
       if best_h == hvals[-1]: eta=min(1.2, eta*1.25)
       elif best_h == hvals[0]: eta=max(0.02, eta*0.65)
       else: eta=min(1.2, eta*1.05)
       # if exact close, validate after accepted? It happens periodically.
       if S - best_trial_S > 1e-5 and it%3==0:
           sc=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
               best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); exact_stall=0
   else:
       # no decrease
       eta=max(0.02, eta*0.5)
       exact_stall +=3  # encourage perturb
       if np.random.random? use rng
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           a=_perturb(a,rng,0)
           v=None
       continue
   # q/alpha anneal
   q=1.0 + 6.0*(1.0 - np.exp(-it/700.0))
   alpha=0.01 + 0.02*(1.0 - np.exp(-it/700.0))
   # if exact_stall moderate, perturb small
   if exact_stall > 80 and exact_stall % 40 ==0:
       a=_perturb(a,rng,1)
       v=None
   if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: break
```

import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:

    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, total_budget - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(4500, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(parent.size)
    if n < 2:
       n=4500; parent=np.ones(n)
    def norm(...): ...
    p0 = norm(parent); if p0 is None: p0=np.ones(n)/n

Let's add `if n > 20000`: maybe too slow; but parent 4498. If hidden uses bigger, dense evaluator O(n^2) impossible. Not worry.

We should not redefine height_sequence_1. We don't.

Let's now craft final code. Ensure final output exactly fenced. No extraneous.

But before final, let's consider if the evaluator's `max_b = max(b_sequence)` for a list of floats uses Python max; for array return? It calls `np.convolve(sequence, sequence)`, sequence list -> numpy array. max okay. It clips sequence to [0,1000]. Our values small. Good.

Potential issue with `evaluate_sequence` if sum_a <0.01 returns inf. Our sum=1. If due to normalization underflow? no.

In `fft_score`, if a has been normalized to sum exactly but due to floor maybe not 1. Score S=2n*maxC independent of sum? If sum changed, exact score uses sum^2. We always normalize after updates, so sum ~1. In line search, normalize. Initial exact normalized. Good.

If trial sum after floor slightly changes, normalize. Good.

Actually `fft_score` doesn't account for sum if not exactly 1. We call only on normalized arrays. In line search, after normalize. Good.

Maybe `np.fft.irfft` returns length `fft_len` with last element? We slice correctly. For score, use first m. For load, conv segment. Good.

One subtle bug: For load, `a[::-1]` and `lam` convolution segment length n. The `lam` length is m=2n-1. The convolution linear length n+m-1 =3n-2. Since `fft_len` >3n-2, okay. Segment `conv[n-1:n-1+n]`. Good.

</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1300.0
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, total_budget - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Base construction / length.  If the pre-imported parent exists, use it.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0:
        parent = np.ones(4500, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if parent.sum() <= 1e-15:
        parent = np.ones(parent.size if parent.size > 0 else 4500, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(parent.size)
    if n < 2:
        n = 4500
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    def _norm(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = x.sum()
        if s <= 1e-15:
            return None
        return x / s

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Quick candidate generation, followed by exact scoring.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p0 = _norm(parent)
    if p0 is None:
        p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    candidates = [p0, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    # Power-law and endpoint-shrunk power-law shapes.
    for al in (0.15, 0.30, 0.45, 0.60, 0.75, 0.90):
        w = (t + 1e-5) ** (-al)
        c = _norm(w)
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

    for al, beta in (
        (0.0, 0.25),
        (0.2, 0.50),
        (0.4, 1.00),
        (0.5, 0.50),
        (0.5, 1.00),
        (0.7, 1.00),
    ):
        w = (t + 1e-5) ** (-al) * (1.0 - t + 1e-5) ** beta
        c = _norm(w)
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

    # Low-order polynomials, kept nonnegative.
    for ctop in (-0.8, -0.4, -0.1, 0.1, 0.4, 0.8):
        w = 1.0 + ctop * t
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.01)
        c = _norm(w)
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

        w = 1.0 + ctop * t + 0.45 * (t - 0.5) ** 2
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.01)
        c = _norm(w)
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

    # Two-level step functions.
    for pfr in (0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70):
        k = max(1, min(n - 1, int(round(pfr * n))))
        for r in (0.25, 0.60, 1.00, 1.50, 2.50, 4.00):
            w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            w[:k] = r
            c = _norm(w)
            if c is not None:
                candidates.append(c)

    # Smooth random mixture candidates.
    for _ in range(18):
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        ncomp = int(1 + rng.integers(0, 4))
        for _ in range(ncomp):
            ctr = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            wid = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.35))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 2.00))
            w += amp * np.exp(-((t - ctr) / wid) ** 2)
        c = _norm(w)
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

    # Blend parent with uniform / reversed parent.
    for bl in (0.20, 0.50, 0.80):
        c = _norm((1.0 - bl) * p0 + bl * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)
        c = _norm((1.0 - bl) * p0 + bl * p0[::-1])
        if c is not None:
            candidates.append(c)

    best_a = p0.astype(np.float64).copy()
    try:
        best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        best_score = np.inf

    for v in candidates[1:]:
        if v is None:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(v.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = v.astype(np.float64).copy()
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
            break

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        try:
            best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            best_score = 2.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fast convolution / subgradient machinery.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    m = 2 * n - 1
    fft_len = 1
    while fft_len < 3 * n - 2:
        fft_len <<= 1

    def _fft_score(a):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
        C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_len)[:m]
        mx = float(np.max(C))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
        return 2.0 * float(n) * mx, C

    def _load(a, lam):
        # load_i = sum_l lam_l a_{l-i}
        Arev = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], fft_len)
        Llam = np.fft.rfft(lam, fft_len)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(Arev * Llam, fft_len)
        return conv[n - 1:n + n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    def _perturb(x, level):
        sig = (0.04, 0.10, 0.22, 0.40)[int(min(level, 3))]
        y = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, x.size))
        if level >= 1 and rng.random() < 0.55:
            b = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            y = (1.0 - b) * y + b * y[::-1]
        if level >= 2 and p0 is not None:
            b = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
            y = (1.0 - b) * y + b * p0
        y = _norm(y)
        if y is None:
            y = x.copy()
        return y

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main local search: natural-gradient / multiplicative-weight updates
    # with adaptive subgradient weights, line search, and restarts.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    a = best_a.astype(np.float64).copy()
    S, C = _fft_score(a)

    eta = 0.18
    q = 2.0
    alpha = 0.02
    v = None
    it = 0
    exact_stall = 0
    no_decrease = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()

        S, C = _fft_score(a)

        # Periodic exact scoring of the current iterate.
        if it % 20 == 1 or S < best_score * 0.999:
            try:
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        exact_stall = 0
                    else:
                        exact_stall += 1
            except Exception:
                exact_stall += 1

        # If current neighborhood has wandered too far from the best known.
        if S > float(best_score) + 0.05 and it % 50 == 0:
            a = best_a.copy()
            a = _perturb(a, 1)
            S, C = _fft_score(a)
            v = None
            no_decrease = 0
            exact_stall = max(exact_stall, 30)

        # Strong restart if the exact score has been stuck for a while.
        if exact_stall > 350 and now < deadline - 30.0:
            a = best_a.copy()
            a = _perturb(a, 2)
            S, C = _fft_score(a)
            v = None
            no_decrease = 0
            exact_stall = 80
            eta = min(1.2, max(eta, 0.35))

        # Small adaptive perturbations before loss of progress becomes severe.
        if exact_stall > 120 and exact_stall % 40 == 30 and now < deadline - 20.0:
            a = _perturb(a, 1)
            S, C = _fft_score(a)
            v = None

        if C.size == 0:
            break

        mx = float(np.max(C))
        if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
            break

        # Subgradient weights: sharper powers focus on active lags,
        # alpha keeps an all-lag baseline so the idea does not blind fail.
        z = np.maximum(C, 0.0) / mx
        lam = z ** q + alpha
        ls = lam.sum()
        if ls <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ls):
            lam = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            lam = lam / ls

        load = _load(a, lam)

        # Natural-gradient direction on the log simplex.
        # A slower phase occasionally uses the plain load direction, which
        # helps escape low-coordinate local minima.
        mode = (it // 80) % 2
        if mode == 0:
            g = a * load
        else:
            g = load.copy()
        g -= g.mean()

        sc90 = float(np.percentile(np.abs(g), 90))
        if sc90 <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sc90):
            g = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            g -= g.mean()
            sc90 = float(np.percentile(np.abs(g), 90)) or 1.0
        g = g / sc90
        g = np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)

        if v is None:
            v = g.copy()
        else:
            v = 0.70 * v + 0.30 * g
            v -= v.mean()
            vs90 = float(np.percentile(np.abs(v), 90))
            if vs90 > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(vs90):
                v = v / vs90
            else:
                v = g.copy()
            v = np.clip(v, -8.0, 8.0)

        # Backtracking / multi-step line search on the FFT proxy score.
        hvals = [eta * 0.30, eta * 0.60, eta, eta * 1.60, eta * 2.50]
        best_trial = None
        best_trial_S = float(S)
        best_h = None

        for h in hvals:
            if h <= 1e-12:
                continue
            trial = a * np.exp(-h * v)
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 1e-18)
            ts = trial.sum()
            if ts <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(ts):
                continue
            trial = trial / ts
            trial_S, _ = _fft_score(trial)
            if trial_S < best_trial_S - 1e-13:
                best_trial_S = float(trial_S)
                best_trial = trial
                best_h = h

        if best_trial is not None:
            a = best_trial
            no_decrease = 0

            if best_h == hvals[-1]:
                eta = min(1.35, eta * 1.25)
            elif best_h == hvals[0]:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.65)
            else:
                eta = min(1.35, eta * 1.04)

            # If the improvement looks meaningful, insist on exact scoring soon.
            if S - best_trial_S > 1e-5 and it % 3 == 0:
                try:
                    sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        exact_stall = 0
                except Exception:
                    pass
        else:
            no_decrease += 1
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.55)
            if no_decrease > 30:
                a = _perturb(a, min(3, 1 + no_decrease // 25))
                v = None
                no_decrease = 0
                exact_stall += 5
            continue

        # Very gentle annealing of the subgradient sharpness and baseline.
        q = 1.0 + 6.0 * (1.0 - np.exp(-it / 700.0))
        alpha = 0.01 + 0.025 * (1.0 - np.exp(-it / 700.0))

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.5:
            break

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact validation and safe return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    result = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    final_score = np.inf
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        final_score = np.inf

    # If something unexpected corrupted the saved best, try the live iterate.
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        try:
            res2 = [float(x) for x in a]
            sc2 = float(evaluate_sequence(res2))
            if np.isfinite(sc2):
                result = res2
                final_score = sc2
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Last-resort valid fallback: uniform gives score 2 and is always valid.
    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > 2.5:
        try:
            if p0 is not None:
                res3 = [float(x) for x in p0]
                sc3 = float(evaluate_sequence(res3))
                if np.isfinite(sc3):
                    result = res3
                    final_score = sc3
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        result = [1.0] * int(n)
        try:
            final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return result
```
